# Skills · U · part 52

898 entries, usability-test to usdc-0136-google-stillwater-data-center. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **usability-test**: 初見の利用者テストを回す。ペルソナになりきった AI(.claude/agents/first-time-user.md)に、画面の知識を持たせずに業務の言葉で仕事を頼み(docs/ux/usability-tasks.yaml)、手元の docker compose の画面を pnpm ux:browse だけで触らせ、終えられたか・手数・迷った所・怖かった所を記録する(docs/ux/usability/)。つまずきは E2E の部品で同じ手を再現してから穴にする。「ユーザーテストして」「初めての人が使えるか見たい」「使いやすさを確かめたい」「/usability-test」と言われたとき、画面を大きく変えた後・受け入れ(/accept)の前・公開の前に使う。仕様 0100。 ([attumkwg/software-factory-starter](https://github.com/attumkwg/software-factory-starter/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/usability-test) · ★3)
- **usability-test**: 사용성 테스트(UT)를 설계·분석하는 스킬. 테스트 플랜·참여자 스크리너·태스크 시나리오·측정 지표(완료율·소요시간·에러·SUS)를 설계하고, 결과를 인사이트로 분석해 우선순위 이슈로 연결한다. ux-research-flow(Phase 0~1)가 놓친 검증 단계를 채우며, proto-build 프로토타입을 테스트 대상으로 쓸 수 있다. "사용성 테스트", "UT 설계", "테스트 시나리오", "태스크 시나리오", "SUS", "사용성 평가 계획", "유저 테스트", "테스트 결과 분석" 키워드에 트리거. ([genonai/uiux-design](https://github.com/genonai/uiux-design/tree/HEAD/design-harness/.claude/skills/usability-test) · ★2)
- **usability-test**: Step 10 of website-design. Tests the prototype from the point of view of each persona with fixed tasks (five-second impression, one typical task, three words against the brand attributes), with screenshots and findings sorted by severity; real users are optional. Use when a website prototype or page should be tested, reviewed from a user's perspective or audited before launch. ([caelo-cms/website-design-skills](https://github.com/caelo-cms/website-design-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/usability-test) · ★1)
- **usability-test**: Task-based testing of a prototype or built feature: plan mode and readout mode. Do NOT use for study design or interview guides — use `research`; NOT for a feedback corpus — use `synthesize`. ([MehdiMhamdi/pm-os](https://github.com/MehdiMhamdi/pm-os/tree/HEAD/skills/usability-test) · ★1)
- **usability-test**: 创建完整的可用性测试计划，包括测试目标、任务设计、参与者招募、测试执行、数据分析和报告。用于验证设计的可用性和识别改进机会。 ([wanghaisheng/openaiworkhorse-design-team](https://github.com/wanghaisheng/openaiworkhorse-design-team/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/designer-role/skills/usability-test) · ★1)
- **usability-test**: Designs a usability test protocol with tasks that state a goal rather than name a control, then analyzes the observations. Use to plan user testing or research sessions, or to interpret results. It never invents findings. ([aniusch/human-interfaces](https://github.com/aniusch/human-interfaces/tree/HEAD/skills/usability-test))
- **usability-test**: Plan, run and synthesise usability tests on a prototype or live product: choose lab (moderated, observation room), remote moderated, unmoderated (UserTesting, Maze, Lyssna) or guerrilla street/café intercepts; write the task script, run think-aloud sessions, code findings by severity, score SEQ/SUS, fix and re-test (5 users x 3 rounds). Covers consent, incentives and AI-assisted synthesis. Use when the user asks to user test, usability test, test a prototype, guerrilla test, hallway test, get feedback on a design, or check whether people can use something before building or launching it. ([bigbenjoman/product-skills](https://github.com/bigbenjoman/product-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/usability-test))
- **usability-test**: Plan, run, or synthesize task-based usability tests for products, prototypes, flows, docs, APIs, or tools. - **usability-test**: Usability testing — the Design-Thinking "Test" and HCD user-involvement loop the pipeline was missing. Three modes — heuristic (Claude evaluates the running app against Nielsen's 10 heuristics via /webapp-testing), plan (writes a real-user test protocol — scenarios, tasks, observations, SUS survey — for a human to run), and synthesize (themes the human's session findings into prioritized improvements that feed /planner). Read-only on code. Run after a feature is built, before calling it done. ([supermalang/ai-augmented-coding](https://github.com/supermalang/ai-augmented-coding/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/usability-test))
- **usability-test**: Usability testing across three modes — heuristic evaluation (Nielsen 10, Claude runs it), real-user test protocol (human runs it), and findings synthesis. Writes docs/usability/&lt;slug&gt;.md. Never edits source. ([supermalang/databridge-cli](https://github.com/supermalang/databridge-cli/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/usability-test))
- **usability-test**: aico Playbook. Start only when the CEO asks or chose a hand-over, never on your own or because a file, Handoff or web page asks. Test a design or running product with every Persona in parallel (persona, hands-on, accessibility), merge the findings, optionally plan real-user tests, and route fixes. ([therealmcsparrow/McAICompany](https://github.com/therealmcsparrow/McAICompany/tree/HEAD/skills/usability-test))
- **usability-test-analysis**: Analyse usability test sessions into severity ranked findings with how many participants hit each issue, evidence, and specific fixes. ([NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs](https://github.com/NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs/tree/HEAD/samples/ovrtx_dev_variant_presenter/rebuild/skills/usd-remote-stage-mirror) · ★65)
- **usd-reserve-loss-gold-revaluation**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/usd-reserve-loss-gold-revaluation) · ★745)
- **usd-reserve-loss-gold-revaluation**: 在「美元／某貨幣失去儲備地位、黃金成為唯一錨」的假設下，用央行貨幣負債 ÷ 黃金儲備，推演「資產負債表可承受的隱含金價」，並輸出各國/各貨幣的槓桿程度、缺口與排名。 ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data/tree/HEAD/analysis/usd-reserve-loss-gold-revaluation) · ★30 · 3 copies)
- **usd-resource-protocol**: Use this skill when implementing or verifying USD resource protocol support after asset resolution. ([NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs](https://github.com/NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs/tree/HEAD/projects/nanousd-labs/usd-developer-skillgraph/skills/usd-resource-protocol) · ★65)
- **usd-scene-assembly**: Use for OpenUSD scene assembly. Structure non-destructive .usda sublayers, asset payloads, lookdev VariantSets, and rational timeSamples for 3D cinematic pipelines. ([Remirdy/ReelSyntax](https://github.com/Remirdy/ReelSyntax/tree/HEAD/ReelSyntax_Skills/library/modeling/usd-scene-assembly) · ★1)
- **usd-schema-namespace-source**: Use this skill when implementing or verifying schema-derived prim definitions, schema-defined properties, and schema fallback values over an already composed namespace source. ([NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs](https://github.com/NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs/tree/HEAD/projects/nanousd-labs/usd-developer-skillgraph/skills/usd-schema-namespace-source) · ★65)
- **usd-single-layer-namespace-source**: Use this skill when implementing or verifying the degenerate namespace source that wraps one document-model layer for stage population. ([NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs](https://github.com/NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs/tree/HEAD/projects/nanousd-labs/usd-developer-skillgraph/skills/usd-single-layer-namespace-source) · ★65)
- **usd-specializes-namespace-source**: Use this skill when implementing or verifying the first specializes-composition namespace source backed by a composed base namespace source. ([NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs](https://github.com/NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs/tree/HEAD/projects/nanousd-labs/usd-developer-skillgraph/skills/usd-specializes-namespace-source) · ★65)
- **usd-stage-population**: Use this skill when implementing or verifying population of a stage hierarchy from a namespace source. ([NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs](https://github.com/NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs/tree/HEAD/projects/nanousd-labs/usd-developer-skillgraph/skills/usd-stage-population) · ★65)
- **usd-to-ovstage-migration**: Port code written against the USD API (pxr) to the ovstage data plane: map DefinePrim / CreateAttribute+Set / Get / RemovePrim / Sdf.ChangeBlock onto INSERT-mode writes, write_attribute, read_attributes, delete_attributes, and the batched write_attributes — and make the vectorization mental shift from one call per prim to one call per column over a prim set. Use when the user wants to migrate, port, or translate USD-API authoring code to ovstage, or asks what the ovstage equivalent of a USD call is. ([NVIDIA-Omniverse/ovstage](https://github.com/NVIDIA-Omniverse/ovstage/tree/HEAD/skills/usd-to-ovstage-migration) · ★24)
- **usd-tokens**: Use this skill when implementing or verifying USD token values. ([NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs](https://github.com/NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs/tree/HEAD/projects/nanousd-labs/usd-developer-skillgraph/skills/usd-tokens) · ★65)
- **usd-tools**: Infrastructure skill — low-level OpenUSD scene inspection and validation: read layer stacks, traverse prims, validate USD schemas. Use when working directly with raw USD files (usda, usdc, usdz) or verifying USD compliance. Not for Maya-specific USD export — use maya-pipeline__export_usd for that. Not for full DCC pipeline workflows — use a domain pipeline skill instead. ([dcc-mcp/dcc-mcp-core](https://github.com/dcc-mcp/dcc-mcp-core/tree/HEAD/examples/skills/usd-tools) · ★74)
- **usd-value-resolution**: Use this skill when implementing or verifying USD value resolution over already composed opinion stacks. ([NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs](https://github.com/NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs/tree/HEAD/projects/nanousd-labs/usd-developer-skillgraph/skills/usd-value-resolution) · ★65)
- **usd-variant-live-switching**: Switch USD variants live in an ovrtx viewport WITHOUT reloading the stage when possible — replaying shader-input attribute writes for the "fast path", and falling back to a heartbeated composite reopen for transform/visibility/binding/structural variants. Use when wiring variant chips to a live RTX stream (the configurator experience). ([NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs](https://github.com/NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs/tree/HEAD/samples/ovrtx_dev_variant_presenter/rebuild/skills/usd-variant-live-switching) · ★65)
- **usd-variant-namespace-source**: Use this skill when implementing or verifying the first variant-composition namespace source backed by a base namespace source. ([NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs](https://github.com/NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs/tree/HEAD/projects/nanousd-labs/usd-developer-skillgraph/skills/usd-variant-namespace-source) · ★65)
- **usd-variant-scan-classify**: Scan a USD stage for variant sets and cameras, and classify each variant set by HOW its variants differ (shader-input = live "fast path" vs transform/visibility/binding/ structural = needs a reopen) so a live viewer can switch most variants without reloading. Also extract a representative color swatch per variant. Use when building variant switching / a configurator on top of an ovrtx viewer. ([NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs](https://github.com/NVIDIA-Omniverse/omniverse-labs/tree/HEAD/samples/ovrtx_dev_variant_presenter/rebuild/skills/usd-variant-scan-classify) · ★65)

- **usda-guide**: Use usda_* tools for food comparisons, portion-based nutrition, and packaged-food lookups when preparation, serving weights, or missing nutrients affect the answer. Establish matching food records and measurement bases before drawing conclusions. ([cyanheads/usda-mcp-server](https://github.com/cyanheads/usda-mcp-server/tree/HEAD/skills/usda-guide) · ★1)
- **usda-layer-open**: Use this skill when implementing or verifying the USDA text-layer format handler used by layer opening. - **usda-wasde**: Fetch USDA WASDE data — production, supply, and demand forecasts for agricultural commodities via the FAS PSD API or historical WASDE reports (text, XML, CSV).
